# Acceptance criteria

Generated by `vault/tools/gen_acs.mjs` from each ticket's Done-when (its row in `vault/tickets/phases/phase-NN.md`); do not hand-edit generated rows. One row per testable sentence, `AC-<ticket>-<n>`. Rows marked *(field check)* are Josh's manual checks at the phase field check, not automated tests. From Phase 1 on, Stage 0 writes the phase's real ACs (PROCESS.md section 5); a ticket whose `acs_source` in `tickets.json` is `stage-0` keeps its hand-written rows here. `vault/tickets/TRACE.md` maps each AC to its ticket and tests.

| AC-EZD-001-2 | EZD-001 Repo and tooling | a sample unit test in the geometry package passes. |

| AC-EZD-002-3 | EZD-002 CI | Preview deploys are checked in EZD-003. |

| AC-EZD-201-2 | EZD-201 Length parse and format | A typed length is stored to the nearest 1/16", halfway going up: 12.33' stores as 147.9375 inches (09j row 10; 03g). |

| AC-EZD-201-4 | EZD-201 Length parse and format | 12 6 is refused everywhere; in wall and room fields 0", 0 1/64", -5", 200' 0 1/16" and 200' 0 1/32" are refused and 200' and 200' 0 1/64" are accepted; other length fields accept 0" (09j rows 9, 13 to 19; Q-0570). |

| AC-EZD-135-1 | EZD-135 System status data and route | Settings → System status is shown only to a user with the System status switch (Owner only by default) and is hidden otherwise (Q-0225; Q-0211; 11 Operations). |
| AC-EZD-135-2 | EZD-135 System status data and route | The page shows job failures (job rows with status failed in the last 30 days, newest first, 50 per page), webhook failures (error_alert kind webhook_failed), the last off-site backup (latest succeeded backup_run of kinds files and db_dump) and the last restore drill (kind restore_drill); a failed test job and a stub backup record appear on it (11 Operations; R12; Q-0664). |
| AC-EZD-135-4 | EZD-135 System status data and route | Job and webhook failures are the active tenant's only; backup and drill rows, which are system-wide, are read through a function that refuses a user without System status (11 Operations; Q-0664). |
| AC-EZD-214-1 | EZD-214 Status and phase switch | The status bar View control switches between Existing and Proposed and sets the status of new objects: a wall drawn in Proposed is New, one drawn in Existing is Existing; the choice is saved on project.phase_default and new projects start Existing (S03 Controls — status bar; S03 Decisions; Q-0664). |
| AC-EZD-214-2 | EZD-214 Status and phase switch | Existing view shows existing objects and hides New ones; Proposed view shows objects to remain plus New ones and hides Demo, as in 09m Phase switch rows 1 to 7 (S03 Decisions; 09m). |
| AC-EZD-214-3 | EZD-214 Status and phase switch | The Phase eye toggles (EZD-218) override the view's display per status; the override is held in the store for the device session (S03 Decisions; Q-0664; 09m rows 5 and 6). |
| AC-EZD-214-4 | EZD-214 Status and phase switch | The wall, door and window panels show a Status select (Existing, Demo, New with the board's dots); several selected objects change at once, shown as Mixed when they differ, as one undo step (Q-0664; Q-0633; S03:115-117). |
| AC-EZD-214-5 | EZD-214 Status and phase switch | A status change is saved with the model and survives a reload; a new room takes the view's default status (03a; 03f; Q-0664). |
| AC-EZD-214-6 | EZD-214 Status and phase switch | Every object stored before phase 6 keeps status Existing and is shown in both views (Q-0558; Q-0630 row 6; Q-0664; 09m row 1). |
| AC-EZD-214-7 | EZD-214 Status and phase switch | For a view-only user the View control changes the display only and nothing is saved (Q-0664; Q-0630 row 9). |
| AC-EZD-214-8 | EZD-214 Status and phase switch | The AI change format gains set_status (ref and status), previewed as an update; an AI remove still acts as the Delete key (Q-0664; Q-0638; Q-0474). |
| AC-EZD-214-9 | EZD-214 Status and phase switch | Switching the View resets the Phase eyes to that view's defaults (Existing: Existing and Demo on, New off; Proposed: Existing and New on, Demo off), and the eyes always show what the canvas shows (Q-0667). |
| AC-EZD-216-1 | EZD-216 Dimensions and text | The Dimension tool (L) places a linear dimension from two clicked points or from a wall face, shown at 1/2" display precision; two points equal within 1/64" place nothing and there is no 200 ft limit (S03 rail; 03g; Q-0664; 09m Dimensions rows 1 to 4). |
| AC-EZD-216-2 | EZD-216 Dimensions and text | An angular dimension shows whole degrees, halfway rounded up (Q-0664; 09m Dimensions rows 5 to 7). |
| AC-EZD-216-3 | EZD-216 Dimensions and text | Typing on a dimension, or double-clicking it, overrides its text (S03 rail; S03 canvas double-click). |
| AC-EZD-216-4 | EZD-216 Dimensions and text | Auto room dimensions (inside width and depth of each room's bounding box) and the auto overall dimension (outer extents of all walls per axis) are placed automatically, can be moved, and update after a wall move with the user's offset kept (S03 rail; Q-0664; 09m row 8). |
| AC-EZD-216-5 | EZD-216 Dimensions and text | The Text tool (T) places a note with an optional leader; notes are 3/32" and labels and dimension text 1/8" on paper, and keep that paper size at every zoom and scale (S03 rail; Q-0664; 09m Text rows 1 to 5). |
| AC-EZD-216-6 | EZD-216 Dimensions and text | The Dimensions overlay eye hides and shows every dimension (S03 Controls — left panel). |
| AC-EZD-216-7 | EZD-216 Dimensions and text | The AI change format gains add_text and add_dimension (Q-0664; Q-0474). |
| AC-EZD-216-8 | EZD-216 Dimensions and text | Dimensions and texts are stored with status and level like every object: a dimension drawn in Proposed is New and hidden in Existing; an auto room dimension takes its room's status and the auto overall dimension is Existing (Q-0667; 03f). |
| AC-EZD-218-1 | EZD-218 Left panel: Layers and Rooms | The left panel has two text tabs, Layers and Rooms, as on board S03; the Versions tab appears when EZD-122 ships; Layers is the default (Q-0664; Q-0500; Q-0673; S03Plan board). |
| AC-EZD-218-2 | EZD-218 Left panel: Layers and Rooms | The Layers tab has Phase (Existing, Demo, New with grey, red and blue dots), Overlays and Rooms sections; each eye hides and shows its objects (S03 Controls — left panel). |
| AC-EZD-218-3 | EZD-218 Left panel: Layers and Rooms | A locked layer's objects cannot be selected by click, box select or Select all; eyes and locks reset on reload (S03 Decisions; Q-0664). |
| AC-EZD-218-4 | EZD-218 Left panel: Layers and Rooms | The Rooms tab lists each room with name and area (for example 165 sf); name, room type (Kitchen, Bath, Laundry, Other), floor finish and ceiling type (flat, single slope, vaulted/cathedral, tray, coffered) can be edited with Edit model; a room type set to Bath persists after reload (S03 Controls — left panel; Q-0664). |
| AC-EZD-218-5 | EZD-218 Left panel: Layers and Rooms | Clicking a room row selects the room and zooms to it; rooms become selectable on the canvas (S03 Controls — left panel; Q-0630 row 8). |
| AC-EZD-218-6 | EZD-218 Left panel: Layers and Rooms | A room stored before phase 6 opens with no type, an empty floor finish and no ceiling type (03b rule 5; Q-0664). |
| AC-EZD-6001-1 | EZD-6001 Floor switcher and floor tools | The floor switcher shows one floor at a time; the canvas draws only that floor's objects (Q-0509; Q-0664). |
| AC-EZD-6001-2 | EZD-6001 Floor switcher and floor tools | A user with Edit model can add and rename a floor; a renamed floor keeps its name after reload (Q-0509; Q-0508). |
| AC-EZD-6001-3 | EZD-6001 Floor switcher and floor tools | Deleting a floor that still has objects asks first and deletes them with it; deleting an empty floor does not ask; the last floor cannot be deleted (Q-0509; Q-0508 Create makes one floor). |
| AC-EZD-6001-4 | EZD-6001 Floor switcher and floor tools | A view-only user can switch floors to view them; add, rename and delete are hidden without Edit model (Q-0667; Q-0630 row 9; S03 Permissions). |
| AC-EZD-219-1 | EZD-219 Status bar popovers | Clicking Snap opens a popover with toggles for endpoints, midpoints, 90/45° and wall faces and an increment of 1/16", 1/8", 1/4", 1/2", 1", 6" or 12" (default 1/2"); changing the increment changes snapping as in 09m Snap rows 1 to 6 (S03 Controls — status bar; Q-0569 (a); Q-0664). |
| AC-EZD-219-2 | EZD-219 Status bar popovers | A snap toggle turned off stops that snap kind (S03 Controls — status bar). |
| AC-EZD-219-3 | EZD-219 Status bar popovers | Clicking Scale opens a popover with 1/8", 3/16", 1/4", 3/8", 1/2", 3/4" and 1" = 1'-0" (default 1/2"); the choice sets what 100% zoom means as in 09m Scale rows 1 to 5, is saved in project.settings, and changes neither geometry nor sheet scale (S03 Controls — status bar; Q-0664). |
| AC-EZD-219-4 | EZD-219 Status bar popovers | The status bar shows live cursor coordinates (S03 Controls — status bar). |
| AC-EZD-219-5 | EZD-219 Status bar popovers | Clicking Zoom % lets the user type a zoom from 5% to 1600%; out-of-range values clamp and empty or non-numeric input is refused, as in 09m Typed zoom rows 1 to 7 (S03 Controls — status bar; Q-0664). |
| AC-EZD-224-1 | EZD-224 Plan thumbnail: storage, routes and project card | A checkpoint captures the already drawn plan canvas to one 440 × 256 WebP under 40,960 bytes (quality 80, then 70, 60, 50, 40), uploads it, and stores its path on model_version.preview_path and project.thumbnail_path; 20 autosaves write none (ticket Done-when; 11 rule 12; Q-0664; 09m Thumbnail rows 2 to 4). |
| AC-EZD-224-2 | EZD-224 Plan thumbnail: storage, routes and project card | Project cards show the image as a lazy image from a signed link without loading editor code, and no plan is drawn on the Projects page (S01 Card thumbnail; 11 rules 7 and 12; Q-0664). |
| AC-EZD-224-3 | EZD-224 Plan thumbnail: storage, routes and project card | An empty model writes no image and the card keeps its empty slot; a checkpoint made on the server without a canvas leaves preview_path null and project.thumbnail_path unchanged (Q-0664; 09m rows 1 and 5). |
| AC-EZD-224-4 | EZD-224 Plan thumbnail: storage, routes and project card | The upload is accepted only for a user with Edit model on that project, only as image/webp, and only at the tenant's own thumb path (Q-0664; 03 rule 1). |
| AC-EZD-235-1 | EZD-235 Derived values | Moving one wall recomputes the areas of its rooms only (the test counts recomputes) (11 rule 2; ticket Done-when). |
| AC-EZD-235-2 | EZD-235 Derived values | A derived value is recomputed only while a view is asking for it (11 rule 2; ticket Scope). |
| AC-EZD-237-1 | EZD-237 Benchmark page | Run benchmark on System status opens the benchmark project, runs the scripted pan, zoom and wall drag, and writes one benchmark_run row with tenant_id, created_by, per-step frames, meanMs, p95Ms, maxMs and over100, memory (null where not exposed), device and app version (03e; R10; Q-0664; 09m Benchmark rows 1 to 4). |
| AC-EZD-237-2 | EZD-237 Benchmark page | The page lists the tenant's earlier runs newest first, 50 per page, to users with System status (Q-0664; 11 rule 9). |
| AC-EZD-224-1 | EZD-224b Plan thumbnail: checkpoint capture and encode | A checkpoint captures the already drawn plan canvas to one 440 × 256 WebP under 40,960 bytes (quality 80, then 70, 60, 50, 40), uploads it, and stores its path on model_version.preview_path and project.thumbnail_path; 20 autosaves write none (ticket Done-when; 11 rule 12; Q-0664; 09m Thumbnail rows 2 to 4). |
| AC-EZD-224-3 | EZD-224b Plan thumbnail: checkpoint capture and encode | An empty model writes no image and the card keeps its empty slot; a checkpoint made on the server without a canvas leaves preview_path null and project.thumbnail_path unchanged (Q-0664; 09m rows 1 and 5). |
| AC-EZD-224-5 | EZD-224b Plan thumbnail: checkpoint capture and encode | The image is the canvas exactly as drawn at the checkpoint (current View, floor and zoom), with no second render (Q-0667; PHASES Slice 6 Risks). |
| AC-EZD-235-3 | EZD-235b Worker pool and the worker check | While a worker job runs, the main thread has no long task over 50 ms (EZD-017 counter) (11 rule 3; Q-0664). |
| AC-EZD-135-1 | EZD-135b System status page, cards and alert link | Settings → System status is shown only to a user with the System status switch (Owner only by default) and is hidden otherwise (Q-0225; Q-0211; 11 Operations). |
| AC-EZD-135-2 | EZD-135b System status page, cards and alert link | The page shows job failures (job rows with status failed in the last 30 days, newest first, 50 per page), webhook failures (error_alert kind webhook_failed), the last off-site backup (latest succeeded backup_run of kinds files and db_dump) and the last restore drill (kind restore_drill); a failed test job and a stub backup record appear on it (11 Operations; R12; Q-0664). |
| AC-EZD-135-3 | EZD-135b System status page, cards and alert link | With no failures and no backup or drill recorded, each section shows its empty state instead of an error (READINESS item 2; PROCESS 3a). |

| AC-EZD-104-1 | EZD-104 Users table | Search filters rows by name or email as typed, ignoring case; no match shows "No users match" (S15 Search users). |
| AC-EZD-104-2 | EZD-104 Users table | Each row shows initials, name, email, the role pill coloured per preset with "(modified)" when the ticked set differs from the role's defaults, EN or ES, "n / 37" and Last active (S15 Row cells; catalog.ts; Q-0695; 09n Switch counts). |
| AC-EZD-104-3 | EZD-104 Users table | A pending invite row shows "Invite pending", "sent <date>", a dashed role pill and Resend; Copy link shows only to the last sender and active Owner-preset members (S15 board; Q-0695; Q-0706). |
| AC-EZD-104-4 | EZD-104 Users table | Up and Down move the selection; the selected row has aria-selected and loads the inspector; with only one member the page shows that row and "Invite your team" (S15 Keyboard; S15 States Empty). |
| AC-EZD-104-5 | EZD-104 Users table | Loading shows skeleton rows; a failed load shows the error state with Retry; without settings_users the Users page shows the in-place no-access line, as built (S15 States; AC-EZD-103-4; Q-0695). |
| AC-EZD-104-6 | EZD-104 Users table | The status bar reads "Users n active · n invited" and "Languages EN n · ES n" from memberships; there is no Sync item before phase 35 (S15 board; Q-0695; 09n Status bar rows 1-2). |
| AC-EZD-104-7 | EZD-104 Users table | last_active_at is written where the session is checked, at most once per 15 minutes per active membership, never failing the request; Last active reads Now, n min ago, n hr ago (same calendar day), Yesterday (previous calendar day) or the date, and "—" before the first touch (Q-0695; Q-0706, which replaces 09n Status bar row 5's 26-hour case; 09n Status bar rows 3, 4, 6-8). |
| AC-EZD-105-1 | EZD-105 Invite user | Invite with a valid new email, name, role (presets, then custom roles), language (default English) and Project access creates an invited membership with the role's switches, and the stub transport records one invite email (S15 Invite user; Q-0029; 20261024100006). |
| AC-EZD-105-2 | EZD-105 Invite user | After Send the dialog shows the invite link with Copy link, and the row appears as Invite pending (S15; R24 #15). |
| AC-EZD-105-3 | EZD-105 Invite user | An invalid email, an email already active or invited in this company, or an email active in another company is refused inline and nothing is written; the refusal never names the other company (S15 Validation; Q-0043; Q-0695). |
| AC-EZD-105-4 | EZD-105 Invite user | The link is valid 7 days from the last send, so it still works after an email scanner has opened it: opening it does not accept it and pressing Accept does; Resend moves the clock and issues a new link and code, and the old ones stop working (Q-0216; Q-0695; 09n Invite expiry). |
| AC-EZD-105-5 | EZD-105 Invite user | Cancel invite deletes the invited membership and its invite; the link and code then refuse; it does not sign the person out of any other company (Q-0695). |
| AC-EZD-105-6 | EZD-105 Invite user | Foreman, Crew and custom roles default to Listed projects; Owner and Designer to All projects (S15 D13; Q-0695; 09n Project scope row 5). |
| AC-EZD-105-7 | EZD-105 Invite user | Accept runs in one database step: it checks the token, checks the email has no other active company, makes the membership active and marks the invite accepted; only then is the session made; a unique index allows one active membership per person (Q-0043; Q-0695). |
| AC-EZD-105-8 | EZD-105 Invite user | user.invited, user.invite_resent, user.invite_cancelled and user.joined (via invite_link) are emitted, and each change writes its event_log row in the same transaction (S15 Events; architecture:440-443; Q-0695). |
| AC-EZD-105-9 | EZD-105 Invite user | Every invite write needs settings_users; the routes answer 403 otherwise (Q-0210; Q-0695). |
| AC-EZD-106-1 | EZD-106 User inspector | Saved switch, role and language changes apply on the user's next request; a scope change ends that user's sessions when the membership is active (current_permissions; 20261026112732; Q-0695). |
| AC-EZD-106-2 | EZD-106 User inspector | The last active member holding settings_users cannot lose it, be deactivated or be removed; a database trigger refuses with last_settings_users_holder after a per-company lock and recount, and the inspector shows the refusal (S15 Validation; Q-0695; 09n Last holder). |
| AC-EZD-106-3 | EZD-106 User inspector | Choosing a role re-ticks all 37 switches to its defaults; the select reads "<Role> (modified)" when the set differs; group counts and "n of 37" update live (S15 Role select; catalog.ts; 09n Switch counts). |
| AC-EZD-106-4 | EZD-106 User inspector | Reset re-ticks to the role without saving; Save and Ctrl/Cmd+S write user_permission and its event_log row in one transaction and emit user_permission.updated as log_only; Save is disabled with no edits; a failed save keeps the edits and shows Retry (S15 Controls; S15 States Error; Q-0695). |
| AC-EZD-106-5 | EZD-106 User inspector | Leaving or changing the selection with unsaved edits asks Save, Discard or Cancel (S15; EZD-103b). |
| AC-EZD-106-6 | EZD-106 User inspector | Deactivate sets status deactivated and ends sessions; Reactivate restores the row unless the person is active in another company; Remove deletes the membership, its switches, listed projects, invite and that person's stored AI key for this company; the app_user row and event_log rows stay; each emits user.deactivated, user.reactivated or user.removed as log_only (S15 More menu; S15 Events; Q-0637; Q-0695; Q-0706). |
| AC-EZD-106-7 | EZD-106 User inspector | Ending sessions on a membership change happens only when the old status was active, so cancelling an invite or removing a deactivated row never signs the person out of another company; the same applies to membership_project changes (20261026112732; 20261019170005; Q-0695). |
| AC-EZD-106-8 | EZD-106 User inspector | Every inspector write needs settings_users and the API refuses others with 403; a holder may edit any row, their own included, and grant any switch (Q-0210; Q-0178; Q-0695). |
| AC-EZD-106-9 | EZD-106 User inspector | A selected pending invite shows invite details, Resend and Cancel invite, plus Copy link for the last sender and active Owner-preset members; its switches can be edited (S15 States; Q-0706). |
| AC-EZD-105b-1 | EZD-105b Owner-set password | "Set password" appears in the row More menu and the inspector for a member with the Owner preset, without settings_users; others never see it and the route answers 403 (Q-0096 P4, P10; Q-0695). |
| AC-EZD-105b-2 | EZD-105b Owner-set password | Set password generates a 16-character password shown once with Copy; the owner cannot type one (Q-0096 P3). |
| AC-EZD-105b-3 | EZD-105b Owner-set password | Setting the password signs the member out on every device and emails them that it was set; the email goes through the stub transport in tests (Q-0096 P5, P7). |
| AC-EZD-105b-4 | EZD-105b Owner-set password | The server-side admin call lives in the one narrow admin file with the invite session call, and an event_log row is written in the same transaction as the change (Q-0695; architecture "Auth and session"). |
| AC-EZD-107-1 | EZD-107 Custom roles | Save as new role, shown only when the user is modified, creates a role with the user's current set and assigns it; that role can then be assigned to another user (S15; phase-01 EZD-107). |
| AC-EZD-107-2 | EZD-107 Custom roles | New role starts from scratch or from a preset; a blank name or a name already used in the company (ignoring case) is refused (S15 Validation). |
| AC-EZD-107-3 | EZD-107 Custom roles | Preset cards open read-only; editing a custom role's switches changes its defaults only and not its holders' rows; Delete is offered only when no member holds the role (Q-0695; role_preset_guard). |
| AC-EZD-107-4 | EZD-107 Custom roles | Role create, edit and delete need settings_users and write role.created, role.updated and role.deleted event_log rows in the same transaction (S15 Events; Q-0695). |
| AC-EZD-109-1 | EZD-109 Event log | A permission change writes an event_log row with company, actor, actor name, action, object type and id, details, time and the request IP (the first x-forwarded-for value as a full address, or null) (03c; 11 Operations; Q-0695). |
| AC-EZD-109-2 | EZD-109 Event log | Only a definer function writes event_log and no API role reads it in v1; rows are kept for the life of the company (Q-0695; architecture:441). |
| AC-EZD-109-3 | EZD-109 Event log | Permission, role, membership, scope and invite changes write their row in the same transaction as the change, so a failed log write fails the change; every other server event goes through the default sink, which reports a failed write and never undoes the action (S15:84; Q-0695). |
| AC-EZD-109-4 | EZD-109 Event log | A system action with no signed-in person writes actor null (Q-0695). |
| AC-EZD-129-1 | EZD-129 User project scope | A user limited to one listed project sees only that project on Projects and gets zero rows for any other project id from the API (phase-01 EZD-129; RLS 20261019170006:34; 09n Project scope row 1). |
| AC-EZD-129-2 | EZD-129 User project scope | The inspector and the Invite dialog offer All projects or Listed projects with a project picker; Save writes membership.scope and membership_project rows (S15 D13). |
| AC-EZD-129-3 | EZD-129 User project scope | The New project wizard's Foreman picker lists active members; picking one adds the project to their list when their scope is Listed (Q-0397; Q-0202). |
| AC-EZD-129-4 | EZD-129 User project scope | Listed with zero projects saves, and that user sees an empty Projects page; adding a project to the list applies on the next request without a sign-out (Q-0695; 09n Project scope rows 2 and 4). |
| AC-EZD-138-1 | EZD-138 Invite link and code for the sender | The person who last sent the invite and active members with the Owner preset see the invite link and 6-digit code on the Users page until it is accepted, cancelled or expired; no other member sees them (Q-0216; Q-0695). |
| AC-EZD-138-2 | EZD-138 Invite link and code for the sender | The invitee enters email and code under "Have an invite code?" on the sign-in page and lands on Projects signed in; the invite is accepted and the code then refuses (Q-0216; Q-0695). |
| AC-EZD-138-3 | EZD-138 Invite link and code for the sender | No everyday sign-in code or link of any user is shown on any page or in any API response (Q-0035). |
| AC-EZD-138-4 | EZD-138 Invite link and code for the sender | Five wrong codes lock the code until Resend while the link still works; the sign-in per-IP limit also applies; an unknown email, an email with no invite and a wrong code all get the same one answer (Q-0695; 09n Invite code tries). |
| AC-EZD-138-5 | EZD-138 Invite link and code for the sender | The link and code are worked out from a per-invite random value with a server key and shown again on the pending row; the invite is looked up by the link's sha-256; no plain hash of the 6-digit code is stored (Q-0695). |

## Phase 8

| AC | Ticket | Criterion |
| --- | --- | --- |
| AC-EZD-116-1 | EZD-116 Defaults framework | A row whose stored value and registry default are both empty reads "Not set", and a feature that needs it shows "Set in Defaults" (S15d:56; board S15d; 09o Effective value). |
| AC-EZD-116-2 | EZD-116 Defaults framework | One typed registry (key, group, label, type, unit, default, per-project flag) lists every S15d row plus the Walls rows, the Snap increment and the two version-cleanup keys; the page renders from it in the board's groups, with no Keyboard shortcuts card (S15d; Q-0558; Q-0664; Q-0697). |
| AC-EZD-116-3 | EZD-116 Defaults framework | /settings/defaults and its save route need settings_defaults; without it the nav item and page are absent and the route answers 403; any active member can read the effective values (Q-0210; Q-0223; Q-0697). |
| AC-EZD-116-4 | EZD-116 Defaults framework | A length accepts 12'6", 12' 6 1/2", 150" and 12.5' and refuses "12 6" with the formats message; a percent outside 0 to 100, minutes outside 1 to 1,440, days outside 1 to 365 (0 allowed for version cleanup age and grace), a seating limit not above the standard overhang and a share-link reminder lead not below the share-link expiry are refused inline and nothing saves (S15d:43-47, 60-62; Q-0561; Q-0697; Q-0706; 09o). |
| AC-EZD-116-5 | EZD-116 Defaults framework | Save is disabled with no edits; Save writes only the edited keys, emits tenant.defaults_updated and shows the saved value after reload; Discard restores the saved values (S15d:52,66; board S15d). |
| AC-EZD-116-6 | EZD-116 Defaults framework | Search defaults filters rows by label across groups and clears back to the full page (board S15d). |
| AC-EZD-116-7 | EZD-116 Defaults framework | Creating a project stores, under project.settings.defaults, the effective company values of the per-project keys (waste factors, four overhangs, cabinet auto-fill and countertop increments), worked out from the registry by the route; a later company change leaves the copy alone (S15d Decision 4; Q-0697; 09o Copy at creation). |
| AC-EZD-116-8 | EZD-116 Defaults framework | The editor session and the Settings page carry the effective defaults, and the editor re-reads them on the project channel resync (Q-0697; model.ts:350). |
| AC-EZD-116-9 | EZD-116 Defaults framework | Loading, failed load with Retry and offline read-only states follow the Settings shell (S15d:56; Q-0214). |
| AC-EZD-125-1 | EZD-125 Edit lock service | API test: an unanswered request releases the lock when its deadline passes, with a checkpoint of the holder's saved work first and lock.auto_released written; the test backdates deadline_at (S06:76; 03b rule 7; Q-0697). |
| AC-EZD-125-2 | EZD-125 Edit lock service | Two simultaneous Start editing calls give the lock to exactly one user; the other is told who holds it (03b rule 6; S06 Decision 4). |
| AC-EZD-125-3 | EZD-125 Edit lock service | After a release lock_epoch is higher, and a save carrying the old epoch is refused with stale_lock_epoch; a re-acquire by the holder from a second tab or device leaves the epoch unchanged (03b rule 6; Q-0697; 09o lock_epoch). |
| AC-EZD-125-4 | EZD-125 Edit lock service | Opening a project whose lock is free gives it to a user with Edit model; a user without Edit model, or anyone while the lock is held, opens view only (04:90; S03:16-17). |
| AC-EZD-125-5 | EZD-125 Edit lock service | Request edit needs Edit model; the holder's own request, a second pending request by the same user and a request without Edit model are refused; a request on a free lock answers that it is free; Cancel removes the pending request (S06:20,75; Q-0697). |
| AC-EZD-125-6 | EZD-125 Edit lock service | Release, Keep editing and Stop editing succeed only for the holder, as one conditional write under the edit_lock row lock; a Keep that arrives after the deadline loses to the auto release; after Keep the requester may ask again at once (S06:21, Decision 5; Q-0697). |
| AC-EZD-125-7 | EZD-125 Edit lock service | The deadline is requested_at plus the Defaults minutes read at request time; a later Defaults change does not move it; the sweep, every lock read and every save apply it on the database clock; a save that finds it expired releases, then returns stale_lock_epoch (S15d:45; Q-0188; Q-0697; 09o Auto-release deadline). |
| AC-EZD-125-8 | EZD-125 Edit lock service | Stop editing saves, makes a checkpoint and then releases; a failed checkpoint keeps the lock and returns the error; a tab-close release is refused while that device's outbox has entries (Q-0681; S06 Decision 12; 03b rules 7, 9; Q-0697). |
| AC-EZD-125-9 | EZD-125 Edit lock service | Every lock change is broadcast on the project channel with ids and timestamps only, and writes its event_log row (actor, IP; auto release actor system) in the same transaction (architecture Realtime; 11:80; Q-0697). |
| AC-EZD-125-10 | EZD-125 Edit lock service | edit_lock and edit_request rows cascade with the project; no route lets anyone but the holder release (Q-0697). |
| AC-EZD-126-1 | EZD-126 Edit lock UI | The Edit lock acceptance test passes in two browsers: the viewer sees the amber banner with the holder's name and since time and Request edit; the holder gets the prompt; no answer releases; the banner turns green (12 Edit lock; board S06). |
| AC-EZD-126-2 | EZD-126 Edit lock UI | Request edit becomes "Requested" with its time and Cancel request; the holder's modal shows the requester, Release, Keep editing and a live minutes:seconds countdown; Keep returns the viewer's button to idle with a toast (S06:75-76; Q-0697). |
| AC-EZD-126-3 | EZD-126 Edit lock UI | The green "Available to edit" toast shows to every member in the project; Start editing appears only with Edit model and stays until used; without it the toast clears after 5 s; the first press gets the lock and the others see the new holder (S06:77-78; Q-0058; Q-0697). |
| AC-EZD-126-4 | EZD-126 Edit lock UI | Stop editing shows only to the holder, icon-only on narrow screens; the status bar shows the lock icon and "View only" while not holding (04:26; Q-0055; S06:80). |
| AC-EZD-126-5 | EZD-126 Edit lock UI | Presence avatars show the members in the project, with an amber ring on the holder and the name on hover; names come from a route, not the broadcast (S06:81; architecture Realtime). |
| AC-EZD-126-6 | EZD-126 Edit lock UI | All lock UI updates arrive over the project channel with no polling (11 rule 11; architecture "No polling lint"). |
| AC-EZD-126-7 | EZD-126 Edit lock UI | After an auto release the former holder is told that unsaved changes stay on the device; a save refused because the same person's other device released gets its own message (Q-0206; Q-0502; Q-0697). |
| AC-EZD-128-1 | EZD-128 Project switcher | The header project name opens the switcher; picking a project opens it in the editor (04:21; S01:8). Menu look and count per Q-0698. |
| AC-EZD-128-2 | EZD-128 Project switcher | Recent projects are the ones this user opened, newest first, stored at editor load; archived projects and projects outside the user's scope never appear in recent or search (Q-0697; Q-0202; S01:53). |
| AC-EZD-128-3 | EZD-128 Project switcher | Switching away while holding the lock drains the outbox, makes a checkpoint and releases before the other project opens; a failure keeps the lock and shows Retry (S06 Decision 12; Q-0681; Q-0697). |
| AC-EZD-128-4 | EZD-128 Project switcher | A search with no match reads one short line; a failed list shows Retry inside the menu (Q-0214). |
| AC-EZD-130-1 | EZD-130 Project settings dialog | Changing a company default leaves an existing project's copied value unchanged (S15d Decision 4). |
| AC-EZD-130-2 | EZD-130 Project settings dialog | Changing a copied value in Project settings changes only that project; other projects and the company default are unchanged; a project with no copy of a key shows the company value and saves a copy (EZD-130 row; Q-0697). |
| AC-EZD-130-3 | EZD-130 Project settings dialog | Name, address and scope of work are editable with Edit model on the project; type and the copied defaults need Edit model and the lock, checked again in the database by lock epoch; fields the user may not change are read-only text and Save is hidden (Q-0202; R24 #16; Q-0697). |
| AC-EZD-130-4 | EZD-130 Project settings dialog | Changing the client needs Company settings or Create projects, as the customers route does; the read-only view shows the client name only, and email and phone only to holders of Company settings, Create projects or Send to client (Q-0400; Q-0697). |
| AC-EZD-130-5 | EZD-130 Project settings dialog | Code edition and Submittal profile are absent until EZD-613 and EZD-712, and nothing is stored for them (Q-0399). |
| AC-EZD-130-6 | EZD-130 Project settings dialog | Validation matches the New project dialog (name and address required, type one of three, client from the customer list) and lengths and percents follow AC-EZD-116-4; Save emits project.updated and the header updates at once; a failed save keeps the dialog open with a Try again message (create_project; Q-0203; Q-0697). Look per Q-0699. |
| AC-EZD-225-1 | EZD-225 Editor states | The canvas skeleton shows while the project loads, and tools stay disabled until the model loads (S03:139). |
| AC-EZD-225-2 | EZD-225 Editor states | A user without Edit model sees no drawing tools and no Request edit; Select, pan, zoom and the read-only inspector remain (S03:145; Q-0630 row 9; Q-0697). |
| AC-EZD-225-3 | EZD-225 Editor states | A locked project shows the amber banner and the drawing tools paused at 55% with "Editing tools paused while view only"; selecting, measuring and Check still work (S06:48,100; Q-0697). |
| AC-EZD-225-4 | EZD-225 Editor states | Clicking a red hosted item shows "Not on a wall" in the inspector (Q-0630 row 27; regression of EZD-204b). |

| AC-EZD-111-4 | EZD-111 Send dialog with Copy link | A send writes one sent_package row (company, project, version, kind client, recipients, note, token hash, expires_at) and one email through the transport; the raw token appears in no row, job payload, notification, log or error report (03d; R14; 03b rule 11; Q-0700). |
| AC-EZD-111-5 | EZD-111 Send dialog with Copy link | The recipient is the project's client name and email, read-only in the dialog and read on the server at send time; a recipient in the request is ignored (Q-0700). |
| AC-EZD-111-6 | EZD-111 Send dialog with Copy link | When the transport fails, the row keeps email_sent_at null, and the dialog still shows Copy link and says the email did not go (architecture Email; Q-0700). |
| AC-EZD-111-7 | EZD-111 Send dialog with Copy link | A send by someone who does not hold the lock uses a server checkpoint, leaves lock_epoch and the holder unchanged, and shows the 03b rule 8 notice with the holder's name and the newest saved object's time (03b rule 8; Q-0700). |
| AC-EZD-111-8 | EZD-111 Send dialog with Copy link | The link is /c/<token>; the server resolver returns the sent package for its token and nothing for a wrong, expired, revoked or deleted-project token; expiry is the Defaults share link expiry (30 days); no client page exists until EZD-1202 (03b rule 11; S15d:46; Q-0700; 09p Token). |
| AC-EZD-111-9 | EZD-111 Send dialog with Copy link | A send never changes project.status (Q-0199; Q-0700). |
| AC-EZD-121-1 | EZD-121 Sent versions and numbering | Two client sends give Version 1 and Version 2, a third gives Version 3, and there is no cap (R24 #26; 09p Numbering). |
| AC-EZD-121-2 | EZD-121 Sent versions and numbering | Once sent, a version's model_json, is_sent, version_no, sent_to and sent_at cannot change and the row cannot be deleted by any caller, service_role included, except by the project Delete cascade (03b rule 7; S20 Version rules; Q-0700). |
| AC-EZD-121-3 | EZD-121 Sent versions and numbering | Two sends started at the same moment get different numbers: numbering happens in the send function under the edit_lock row lock, with a unique index (Q-0700). |
| AC-EZD-121-4 | EZD-121 Sent versions and numbering | Every send writes a fresh checkpoint from the saved rows, even with no change since the last sent version (03a; R24 #16b). |
| AC-EZD-121-5 | EZD-121 Sent versions and numbering | The header reads "Working copy" before any send, "Working copy · Version N sent" after, and "Version N" while that version is open read-only; the "Rev N issued" forms are built but unreachable until EZD-717 (EZD-121 text; S03 board; Q-0700). |
| AC-EZD-121-6 | EZD-121 Sent versions and numbering | The project card reads "Version N" after a send and "Working copy" before (S01 Data shown; Q-0700). |
| AC-EZD-121-7 | EZD-121 Sent versions and numbering | version.sent is broadcast on the project channel and writes an event_log row (S20 Events; EZD-109). |
| AC-EZD-122-1 | EZD-122 Versions panel | Revert creates a new working version and leaves sent versions unchanged (S20 Revert). |
| AC-EZD-122-2 | EZD-122 Versions panel | The Versions tab appears in the left panel from this ticket, with groups Sent, Named snapshots and Working versions; each row shows label or date and time, cause and author (board S20; Q-0673; Q-0700). |
| AC-EZD-122-3 | EZD-122 Versions panel | Clicking a row opens it read-only on the canvas; the status bar reads "Viewing <label> · read-only"; "Back to working copy" returns (board S20; EZD-225). |
| AC-EZD-122-4 | EZD-122 Versions panel | Name snapshot on a working version needs Edit model (no lock), refuses an empty name, sets is_snapshot and the label, and emits version.snapshot_named (S20; Q-0700). |
| AC-EZD-122-5 | EZD-122 Versions panel | Revert needs Edit model and the lock and a current lock_epoch; it migrates and validates the version's objects, checkpoints the current working copy first, replaces it with the chosen version, writes "Revert from <label>", clears undo and emits version.reverted (Q-0700). |
| AC-EZD-122-6 | EZD-122 Versions panel | Without Edit model the panel shows no Name snapshot and no Revert (S20 Permissions). |

| AC-EZD-123-1 | EZD-123 Version cleanup | An ignored sheet deletes the unticked versions grace days (default 7) after cleanup_shown_at, never in the run that stamps them (so grace 0 deletes at the next run); cleanup.deleted is emitted and logged (R24 #4; Q-0700; Q-0708; 09p Cleanup clock rows 1, 2, 4-6). |
| AC-EZD-123-2 | EZD-123 Version cleanup | Ticked rows become named snapshots with the typed name or the default date and time (S20 Cleanup rows). |
| AC-EZD-123-3 | EZD-123 Version cleanup | The daily run picks rows that are not sent, not snapshot, never shown and at least age days old (default 30), stamps cleanup_shown_at, and sends cleanup_due in the app and by email: one per project to each active member with Edit model whose scope includes the project (Q-0700). |
| AC-EZD-123-4 | EZD-123 Version cleanup | Age and grace are two Defaults keys, whole days, 0 allowed (S15d:44; Q-0697; PHASES slice 9 step 5). |
| AC-EZD-123-5 | EZD-123 Version cleanup | Opening a project with due rows shows the sheet to users with Edit model, with the board's title, note, Select All / None, "N keep · N delete", preview, date, author, cause, change summary, Keep, Name, delete date, Later and "Keep N, delete N unticked" (board S20). |
| AC-EZD-123-6 | EZD-123 Version cleanup | Save choices needs Edit model and the lock and deletes only rows that are neither snapshot nor sent; someone without the lock sees Later only (board footer; Q-0700). |
| AC-EZD-123-7 | EZD-123 Version cleanup | The job never deletes a sent or named row or a row younger than age, and the trigger refuses such deletes regardless (Q-0700). |
| AC-EZD-123-8 | EZD-123 Version cleanup | With dryRun the handler lists what it would delete and writes nothing; the gate runs it first; it is safe to run twice (PHASES slice 9 risk; Q-0700). |
| AC-EZD-123-9 | EZD-123 Version cleanup | The change summary reads "N added · N changed · N removed" against the parent version, or "First version" when there is none (Q-0700). |
| AC-EZD-123-10 | EZD-123 Version cleanup | The preview is model_version.preview_path from EZD-224; a null path shows a placeholder (Q-0664). |

| AC-EZD-124-2 | EZD-124 Project card menu | Archive sets archived_at: the project leaves every tab, count and search and appears under Archived; Restore clears it (Q-0201). |

| AC-EZD-2000-1 | EZD-2000 Gate rules that keep pages small and fast as the app grows | The gate enforces these rules (new conventions-check rules C12+ in scripts/conventions/check.mjs, eslint rules, or scripts/perf gate rows, each with a test that proves it fails on a bad example): (1) every app route has a download budget row and is measured at its real page, so a new route fails the gate until it has one; routes without a spec number get their measured size plus 10% as a ceiling, recorded in vault/build/assumptions.md; (2) heavy libraries (full zod, three, Supabase realtime, the image encoder, PDF code) are not in any page's first-load bundle except where an existing ENTRY_RULES allowance says so (extend eslint-rules/boundaries.mjs and the contents.* rows); (3) a server page or layout does not await two independent data reads one after another (a lint rule that flags sequential awaits whose later call does not use the earlier result, with an escape comment that must name the reason); (4) pages inline only their own messages (the EZD-1995 cap); (5) images and fonts in the repo are compressed (fonts woff2, raster images under a size cap or loaded lazily). |
| AC-EZD-2000-2 | EZD-2000 Gate rules that keep pages small and fast as the app grows | docs/architecture.md describes each rule under Machine checks, and CLAUDE.md's rules point builders at it in one line. Nothing a person sees changes unless this ticket says so; text on a mockup is used as is, any new text follows CLAUDE.md rule 6 (listed in vault/build/assumptions.md).
